## 十一、S19直接读取与非循环规则

```text
S00_TO_S19_SECTION_POSTING_PERMISSION=NO
S00_TO_S19_HASH_BINDING_PERMISSION=NO
S19_DIRECT_RESOLUTION_KEY=CANONICAL_FILENAME_PLUS_CURRENT_RUNTIME_ROOT_MARKER_PLUS_STATIC_PAYLOAD_HASH
S19_CONTROL_TEXT_ASTROLOGICAL_CONTRIBUTION=ZERO
S19_MISSING_ACTION=RELEASE_GATE_UNAVAILABLE_FAIL_CLOSED
```

S19修改不使S00失效；只有S01—S18活动正文变化才使对应索引失效。S19负责绑定S00完整文件哈希和主提示词哈希。

## 十二、检索失败与防绕过

```text
INDEX_FILE_MISSING=SOURCE_RETRIEVAL_EXECUTED_NO
QUERY_PLAN_MISSING=SOURCE_RETRIEVAL_EXECUTED_NO
ONLY_LIBRARY_NAMES_RETURNED=CALL_OBJECT_MISSING
ONLY_SECTION_TITLE_RETURNED=CALL_OBJECT_MISSING
ONLY_SOURCE_ID_RETURNED_WITHOUT_PARENT_TEXT=CALL_OBJECT_INVALID
WINNER_ONLY_QUERY=CALL_DEPTH_SYMMETRY_FAIL
NO_LIMIT_OR_COUNTEREVIDENCE_QUERY=RETRIEVAL_PACKAGE_INCOMPLETE
INDEX_HIT_USED_AS_FACT=ZERO_INDEX_CONTRIBUTION
ACTIVE_FILE_ID_MISMATCH=INDEX_BINDING_STALE_FAIL_CLOSED
ORIGINAL_HASH_MISMATCH=INDEX_BINDING_STALE_FAIL_CLOSED
S19_LOOKUP_THROUGH_S00=CONTROL_ROUTE_INVALID
```

S00结束。
